namespace = ecumenical_council_events

##########################################
## ecumenical_council_events.0001 - Enter passive state - you arrived
## ecumenical_council_events.0002 - The Summons start
## ecumenical_council_events.0010 - The First Debate starts - Host
## ecumenical_council_events.0011 - The First Debate starts - Guest
## ecumenical_council_events.0020 - The Second Debate starts - Host
## ecumenical_council_events.0021 - The Second Debate starts - Guest
## ecumenical_council_events.0031 - The Third Debate starts - Guest
## ecumenical_council_events.0040 - Conclusion event - Host
## ecumenical_council_events.0041 - Conclusion event - Guest
## ecumenical_council_events.0050-0073 - Third Debate per-doctrine-group stance picks (change / keep / strengthen)
## ecumenical_council_events.0080 - Debate vote resolution - Host (tally + apply outcome)
## ecumenical_council_events.0081 - Debate vote resolution - Guest
## ecumenical_council_events.0101-0129 - Themed "sway the group" debate phase pulse events (grouped by a shared attendee feature)
##########################################

# Makes scopes: lead_innovator, lead_conservative and court_chaplain always available in all events
scripted_effect save_major_scopes_effect = {
	save_scope_as = actor # needed for values
	scope:activity = {
		random_in_list = {
			variable = major_innovator
			limit = {
				this != scope:actor
			}
			save_scope_as = lead_innovator
		}
		random_in_list = {
			variable = major_conservative
			limit = {
				this != scope:actor
			}
			save_scope_as = lead_conservative
		}
		# Fallback
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
			}
			ordered_attending_character = {
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:lead_conservative
						}
						this != scope:lead_conservative
					}
					is_clergy = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = lead_innovator
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_conservative }
			}
			ordered_attending_character = {
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:lead_innovator
						}
						this != scope:lead_innovator
					}
					is_clergy = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = lead_conservative
			}
		}
	}
	# Save for portrait
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:lead_innovator.piety >= scope:lead_conservative.piety
		}
		scope:lead_innovator = {
			save_scope_as = lead_portrait
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:lead_conservative = {
			save_scope_as = lead_portrait
		}
	}
	cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
		save_scope_as = court_chaplain
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:court_chaplain }
		}
		ordered_attending_character = {
			order_by = highest_held_title_tier
			limit = {
				this != scope:actor
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:lead_conservative
					}
					this != scope:lead_conservative
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:lead_innovator
					}
					this != scope:lead_innovator
				}
				is_clergy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = court_chaplain
		}
	}
}

# Ecumenical Council themed sway events (ecumenical_council_events.0101-0129):
# shared groupings for each event's trigger gate and immediate selection.
# Evaluated on an attending character; root is the event root (the persuading player).
# Kept as named scripted_triggers because save_sway_guests_scopes_effect takes TRIGGER= via $TRIGGER$.
scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_learned_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_trait = erudite
		has_trait = theologian
		has_trait = lifestyle_mystic
		learning >= high_skill_rating
		has_trait = education_learning_5
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_devout_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_trait = zealous
		has_trait = devoted
		piety_level >= high_piety_level
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_worldly_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_trait = greedy
		has_trait = cynical
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_relation_friend = root
		has_relation_best_friend = root
		has_relation_lover = root
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_has_plague_struck_realm_trigger = {
	any_realm_province = {
		any_province_epidemic = {
			exists = this
		}
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_has_besieged_faithful_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = ec_besieged_ruler
	top_liege ?= {
		any_character_war = {
			primary_defender = scope:ec_besieged_ruler.top_liege
			any_war_attacker = {
				faith != scope:ec_besieged_ruler.faith
			}
		}
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_warrior_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_trait = faith_warrior
		has_trait = holy_warrior
		has_trait = order_member
		has_trait = lifestyle_blademaster
		has_trait = knight_errant
		has_trait = gallant
		has_trait = berserker
		has_trait = shieldmaiden
		has_trait = despoiler_of_byzantium
		any_killed_character = {
			OR = {
				death_reason = death_battle
				death_reason = death_head_ripped_off
				death_reason = death_cloven_in_half
				death_reason = death_viciously_dismembered
				death_reason = death_ripped_apart_limb_by_limb
				death_reason = death_chopped_to_pieces
				death_reason = death_heart_ripped_out
				death_reason = death_fear
				death_reason = death_skull_cracked_open
				death_reason = death_strangled_with_own_intestines
				death_reason = death_decapitated
				death_reason = death_piteously_cut_down
			}
		}
		any_character_trait = {
			has_trait_flag = commander_trait_flag
		}
		martial >= high_skill_rating
		has_trait = education_martial_5
		AND = {
			is_knight = yes
			prowess > decent_skill_rating
		}
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_high_intrigue_trigger = {
	OR = {
		intrigue >= high_skill_rating
		has_trait = education_intrigue_5
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_high_stewardship_trigger = {
	OR = {
		stewardship >= high_skill_rating
		has_trait = education_stewardship_5
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_high_diplomacy_trigger = {
	OR = {
		diplomacy >= high_skill_rating
		has_trait = education_diplomacy_5
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_ailing_trigger = {
	any_character_trait = {
		OR = {
			has_trait_flag = illness
			has_trait_flag = age_related_ailment
		}
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_dynasty_member_trigger = {
	dynasty = root.dynasty
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_same_culture_trigger = {
	culture = root.culture
}

# Attendees whose customs feel distant from root: other culture, plus heritage, language, or realm distance.
scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_distant_custom_trigger = {
	NOT = { culture = root.culture }
	OR = {
		NOT = { culture = { has_same_culture_heritage = root.culture } }
		NOT = { knows_language_of_culture = root.culture }
		top_liege != root.top_liege
	}
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_vassal_trigger = {
	is_vassal_of = root
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_ally_trigger = {
	is_allied_to = root
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger = {
	has_relation_rival = root
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_dreads_root_trigger = {
	has_dread_level_towards = { target = root level >= 1 }
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_young_trigger = {
	age < 30
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_elderly_trigger = {
	age >= 50
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_ambitious_trigger = {
	has_trait = ambitious
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_is_prestigious_trigger = {
	prestige_level >= high_prestige_level
}

scripted_trigger ecumenical_council_has_desired_tenet_trigger = {
	any_desired_tenet = { always = yes }
}

# EXPECTS: event root, scope:activity, TRIGGER= same scripted_trigger passed to save_sway
scripted_effect ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				scope:lead_conservative ?= {
					$TRIGGER$ = yes
				}
			}
		}
		clear_saved_scope = lead_conservative
		scope:activity = {
			ordered_attending_character = {
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					this != root
					is_ai = yes
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
					$TRIGGER$ = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = lead_conservative
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				scope:lead_innovator ?= {
					$TRIGGER$ = yes
				}
			}
		}
		clear_saved_scope = lead_innovator
		scope:activity = {
			ordered_attending_character = {
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					this != root
					is_ai = yes
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					$TRIGGER$ = yes
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:lead_conservative
						}
						this != scope:lead_conservative
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = lead_innovator
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
			scope:host = { $TRIGGER$ = yes }
			scope:host != root
		}
		scope:host = {
			save_scope_as = lead_innovator
		}
	}
}

# Makes scopes: lead_innovator, lead_conservative and court_chaplain always available in all sway guests events, scope actor for tenet popularity values, and saves 2 sizes of the swayed group: full and halved as feature_innovators_size, feature_innovators_size_halved and feature_conservatives_size, feature_conservatives_size_halved.
scripted_effect save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = {
	save_scope_as = actor # needed for values
	scope:activity = {
		var:current_topic ?= { save_scope_as = ec_current_topic }
		every_guest_subset = {
			name = conservative
			if = {
				limit = {
					$TRIGGER$ = yes
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
					# The AI cannot win back attendees already swayed this debate
					trigger_if = {
						limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
						NOT = {
							scope:activity = {
								is_target_in_variable_list = {
									name = locked_into_conservatives
									target = prev
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_list = feature_conservatives
			}
		}
		every_guest_subset = {
			name = innovator
			if = {
				limit = {
					$TRIGGER$ = yes
					scope:host != this
					is_ai = yes
					trigger_if = {
						limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
						NOT = {
							scope:activity = {
								is_target_in_variable_list = {
									name = locked_into_innovators
									target = prev
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_list = feature_innovators
			}
		}
		ordered_in_list = {
			list = feature_conservatives
			order_by = highest_held_title_tier
			limit = {
				this != scope:actor
				is_clergy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = lead_conservative
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_conservative }
			}
			ordered_in_list = {
				list = feature_conservatives
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
				}
				save_scope_as = lead_conservative
			}
		}
		# Do not fall back to unthemed attendees; that cast wrong faces into themed sway events.
		ordered_in_list = {
			list = feature_innovators
			order_by = highest_held_title_tier
			limit = {
				this != scope:actor
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:lead_conservative
					}
					this != scope:lead_conservative
				}
				is_clergy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = lead_innovator
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
			}
			ordered_in_list = {
				list = feature_innovators
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:lead_conservative
						}
						this != scope:lead_conservative
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = lead_innovator
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
				scope:host = { $TRIGGER$ = yes }
				scope:host != root
			}
			scope:host = {
				save_scope_as = lead_innovator
			}
		}

	}
	cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
		save_scope_as = court_chaplain
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:court_chaplain }
		}
		ordered_attending_character = {
			order_by = highest_held_title_tier
			limit = {
				this != scope:actor
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:lead_conservative
					}
					this != scope:lead_conservative
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:lead_innovator
					}
					this != scope:lead_innovator
				}
				is_clergy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = court_chaplain
		}
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = ec_sway_change_tier
		value = ecumenical_council_sway_change_tier_value
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = ec_sway_duel_penalty
		value = ecumenical_council_sway_duel_penalty_value
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = feature_conservatives_size
		value = list_size:feature_conservatives
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = feature_conservatives_size_halved
		value = {
			value = list_size:feature_conservatives
			multiply = 0.5
			floor = yes
		}
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = feature_conservatives_free_max
		value = ecumenical_council_sway_free_max_conservatives_value
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = feature_innovators_size
		value = list_size:feature_innovators
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = feature_innovators_size_halved
		value = {
			value = list_size:feature_innovators
			multiply = 0.5
			floor = yes
		}
	}
}

# Tracks the court chaplain's highest-scoring selectable (non-current) doctrine as scope:ec_chaplain_pick
# Ties intentionally keep the first candidate, matching the visible option order.
scripted_effect ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { scope:ec_debate_topic_3 = doctrine:$DOCTRINE$ }
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = ec_chaplain_candidate_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:$DOCTRINE$ = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = scope:court_chaplain.ecumenical_council_doctrine_desire_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { exists = scope:ec_chaplain_pick }
					scope:ec_chaplain_candidate_value > scope:ec_chaplain_pick_value
				}
			}
			doctrine:$DOCTRINE$ = {
				save_scope_as = ec_chaplain_pick
			}
			save_scope_value_as = {
				name = ec_chaplain_pick_value
				value = scope:ec_chaplain_candidate_value
			}
		}
	}
}

# Enter passive state - you arrived
ecumenical_council_events.0001 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0001
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.desc_intro
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					this = scope:host
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.host
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					this != scope:host 
					OR = {
						has_relation_rival = scope:activity.activity_host
						reverse_opinion = {
							target = scope:activity.activity_host
							value < 0
						}
					}
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.dislikes_host
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					this != scope:host 
					OR = {
						has_relation_rival = scope:activity.activity_host
						reverse_opinion = {
							target = scope:activity.activity_host
							value >= 40
						}
					}
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.likes_host
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					this != scope:host
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.neutral
			}
		}
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					intent_target.location ?= root.location
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.intent_arrived
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { exists = intent_target }
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.waiting_for_intent
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0001.no_intent_target
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				this != scope:host
				opinion = {
					target = scope:activity.activity_host
					value < 0
				}
			}
			animation = boredom
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				this = scope:host
			}
			animation = personality_zealous
		}
		animation = happiness
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = { root != scope:host }
		character = scope:host
		animation = personality_zealous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:center_portrait
		animation = debating
	}
	cooldown = { years = 5 }

	immediate = {
		location = { save_scope_as = my_location }
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = intent_target
			}
			intent_target = { save_scope_as = intent_target }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				involved_activity = {
					any_attending_character = {
						is_adult = yes
						NOR = {
							this = root
							this = scope:host
						}
						is_travelling = no
					}
				}
			}
			involved_activity = {
				random_attending_character = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						NOR = {
							this = root
							this = scope:host
						}
						is_travelling = no
					}
					save_scope_as = center_portrait
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:activity.activity_host
					value < 0
				}
			}
			text = ecumenical_council_events.0001.a.bad
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:activity.activity_host
					value >= 0
				}
			}
			text = ecumenical_council_events.0001.a.good
		}
		add_piety = miniscule_piety_gain
		
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

# The Summons start
ecumenical_council_events.0002 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0002.t

	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_councils_judgement"
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = pam_great_schism_decision_taken }
				situation:the_christian_church ?= {
					situation_current_phase = saeculum_obscurum
				}
			}
			scope:activity = { set_variable = pam_schism_crisis_council }
		}
		involved_activity = {
			activity_tenet:1 = {
				save_scope_as = ec_debate_topic_1
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0002.desc_intro
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0002.reformist
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { scope:activity = { has_variable = pam_schism_crisis_council } }
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.schism_crisis_intro
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0002.first_tenet
		}
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					this = scope:host
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0002.host
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0002.guest
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				this = scope:host
			}
			animation = acknowledging_crozier
		}
		animation = interested
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = { root != scope:host }
		character = scope:host
		animation = acknowledging
	}
	cooldown = { years = 5 }

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0002.a
		
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

# Start of First Debate - Host, decide what you want to say about this tenet
ecumenical_council_events.0010 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0010.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0010.desc.intro
		# What the tenet is about from lead_portrait
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					scope:ec_debate_topic_1 = tenet:tenet_peace_of_god

				"faith.tenet_popularity(scope:ec_debate_topic_1)" > tenet_popularity_neutral_threshold

			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0010.desc.intro_core_demote_bad
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				faith.main_rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
						status = core
					}
				}
				"faith.tenet_popularity(scope:ec_debate_topic_1)" <= tenet_popularity_neutral_threshold
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0010.desc.intro_core_demote_good
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				faith.main_rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
						status = prohibited
					}
				}
				"faith.tenet_popularity(scope:ec_debate_topic_1)" > tenet_popularity_neutral_threshold
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0010.desc.intro_prohibited_promote_good
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				faith.main_rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
						status = prohibited
					}

		}
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = worry
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:court_chaplain
		animation = throne_room_conversation_1
	}

	theme = ecumenical_council

	immediate = {
		# For MP
		ecumenical_council_busy_effect = yes
		save_major_scopes_effect = yes
		scope:ec_debate_topic_1 = {
			save_scope_as = current_topic
		}
		random_character_tenet = {
			status = core
			faith_filter = root.faith
			save_scope_as = current_core_1
		}
		random_character_tenet = {
			status = core
			faith_filter = root.faith
			limit = {
				this != scope:current_core_1
			}
			save_scope_as = current_core_2
		}
		random_character_tenet = {
			status = core
			faith_filter = root.faith
			limit = {
				this != scope:current_core_1
				this != scope:current_core_2
			}
			save_scope_as = current_core_3
		}
	}

	# Core
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0010.a_1
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:current_core_1
			scope:ec_debate_topic_1 != scope:current_core_1
			faith.main_rite = {
				rite_can_choose_tenet = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
			}
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			exists = scope:current_core_1
			scope:ec_debate_topic_1 != scope:current_core_1
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			ecumenical_council_make_core_preview_effect = {
				DEMOTED = scope:current_core_1
				PROMOTED = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
			}
		}
		scope:activity = {
			set_variable = {
				name = core_tenet_to_swap_1
				value = scope:current_core_1
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = flag:make_core }
		}
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 10
			add = scope:current_core_1.pam_tenet_unpopularity_impact_value
			add = scope:ec_debate_topic_1.pam_tenet_popularity_impact_value
			add = scope:ec_debate_topic_1.pam_christian_vice_restraint_value
			# Go in increments, don't jump to the extreme status immediately
			if = {
				limit = {
					faith.main_rite = {
						has_tenet_status = {
							tenet = scope:ec_debate_topic_1
							status = prohibited
						}
					}
				}
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

# Start of Third Debate - Guest, this is what the host decided we're debating
ecumenical_council_events.0031 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0031.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0031.desc.intro
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0031.desc.proposal
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:host
		animation = acknowledging
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council

	immediate = {
		ecumenical_council_busy_effect = yes
		scope:activity = {
			var:third_debate_proposal ?= { save_scope_as = ec_proposed_doctrine }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0031.a
		ecumenical_council_log_stance_effect = { SIDE = innovator }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0031.b
		ecumenical_council_log_stance_effect = { SIDE = conservative }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

# Conclusion event - Host
ecumenical_council_events.0040 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0040.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0040.desc
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						scope:activity.var:first_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
						scope:activity.var:second_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
						scope:activity.var:third_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed

				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0040.desc.change

			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0040.desc.status_quo
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { scope:activity = { has_variable = pam_schism_crisis_council } }
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.schism_crisis_end
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = thinking
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0040.a
		disburse_ecumenical_council_activity_rewards = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

# Debate vote resolution - Host. Tallies the innovator and conservative guest subsets,
# applies the proposed change when innovators are at least as numerous as conservatives,
# then dispatches the guest resolution event.
ecumenical_council_events.0080 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0080.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:passed }
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0080.desc.passed
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0080.desc.failed
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:ec_current_topic
				NOT = { exists = scope:activity.var:third_debate_resolution }
			}
			desc = {
				# Mechanical tenet outcome line: topic + proposed status.
				first_valid = {
					triggered_desc = {
						trigger = {
							scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:passed
							scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:make_core
							exists = scope:core_tenet_to_swap
						}
						desc = ecumenical_council_events.0080.desc.outcome.passed.make_core
					}
					triggered_desc = {
						trigger = {
							scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:passed
							OR = {
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:permit
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:prohibit
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:learn
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:make_core
							}
						}
						desc = ecumenical_council_events.0080.desc.outcome.passed
					}
					triggered_desc = {
						trigger = {
							OR = {
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:permit
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:prohibit
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:learn
								scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal ?= flag:make_core
							}
						}
						desc = ecumenical_council_events.0080.desc.outcome.failed
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_zealous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_portrait
		animation = preaching_no_book
	}

	immediate = {
		save_major_scopes_effect = yes
		scope:activity = {
			var:current_topic ?= { save_scope_as = ec_current_topic }
			every_guest_subset = {
				name = innovator
				add_to_list = ec_innovators
			}
			every_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
				add_to_list = ec_conservatives
			}
		}
		# Saved values, not activity variables: the tally is only read by loc, and an unused variable is a startup error; 0081 inherits them
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = ec_vote_for
			value = list_size:ec_innovators
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = ec_vote_against
			value = list_size:ec_conservatives
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:activity.var:core_tenet_to_swap_2 }
			scope:activity.var:core_tenet_to_swap_2 = { save_scope_as = core_tenet_to_swap }
		}
		else = {
			scope:activity.var:core_tenet_to_swap_1 ?= { save_scope_as = core_tenet_to_swap }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { list_size:ec_innovators >= list_size:ec_conservatives }
			scope:activity = { set_variable = { name = last_debate_passed value = flag:passed } }
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal }
				scope:activity = {
					add_activity_log_entry = {
						key = ecumenical_council_debate_outcome_log
						score = 80
						tags = { completed }
						show_in_conclusion = yes
						character = scope:host
						location = scope:host.location
						ecumenical_council_apply_debate_outcome_effect = yes
					}
				}
				# Announced once per council, not once per debate; the letter covers the rest at the end.
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:activity.var:ec_ruling_announced } }
					scope:activity = {
						set_variable = {
							name = ec_ruling_announced
							value = yes
						}
					}
					# Only players engaged with the Christian Church.
					every_player = {
						limit = {
							faith = scope:host.faith
							NOT = { involved_activity ?= scope:activity }
							any_character_situation = {
								this = situation:the_christian_church
							}
						}
						send_interface_message = {
							type = msg_ecumenical_council_ruling
							left_icon = scope:host
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:activity = {
				add_activity_log_entry = {
					key = ecumenical_council_debate_outcome_log
					score = 80
					tags = { completed }
					show_in_conclusion = yes
					character = scope:host
					location = scope:host.location
					set_variable = { name = last_debate_passed value = flag:failed }
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { exists = var:first_debate_resolution }
						}
						set_variable = {
							name = first_debate_resolution
							value = flag:failed
						}
						custom_tooltip = first_debate_resolultion_failed_tt
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { exists = var:second_debate_resolution }
						}
						set_variable = {
							name = second_debate_resolution
							value = flag:failed
						}
						custom_tooltip = second_debate_resolultion_failed_tt
					}
					else = {
						set_variable = {
							name = third_debate_resolution
							value = flag:failed
						}
						custom_tooltip = third_debate_resolultion_failed_tt
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0080.a
		custom_tooltip = ecumenical_council_vote_tally_tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			ecumenical_council_current_debate_personal_tenet_fulfillment_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	after = {
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					this != scope:host

				}
				trigger_event = ecumenical_council_events.0081
			}
		}
	}
}

# Debate vote resolution - Guest. Shows the outcome from the guest's side, branching on
# whether they argued with the innovators or the conservatives and whether the vote passed.
ecumenical_council_events.0081 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0081.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:passed
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0081.desc.innovator_passed
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:failed
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0081.desc.innovator_failed
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
					scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:passed
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0081.desc.conservative_passed
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
					scope:activity.var:last_debate_passed ?= flag:failed
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0081.desc.conservative_failed
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0081.desc.neutral
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:host
		animation = acknowledging
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0081.a
		custom_tooltip = ecumenical_council_vote_tally_tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			ecumenical_council_current_debate_personal_tenet_fulfillment_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

# The chaplain's account of a council their liege sat out, sent whether or not anything changed.
ecumenical_council_events.0082 = {
	type = letter_event
	opening = ecumenical_council_events.0082.opening
	sender = scope:ec_reporter

	immediate = {
		scope:ec_council = {
			activity_tenet:1 ?= { save_scope_as = ec_letter_tenet_1 }
			activity_tenet:2 ?= { save_scope_as = ec_letter_tenet_2 }
			activity_doctrine:1 ?= { save_scope_as = ec_letter_doctrine_1 }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					scope:ec_council ?= {
						NOR = {
							var:first_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
							var:second_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
							var:third_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
						}
					}
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0082.desc.unchanged
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0082.desc
		}
	}

	# The letter stays in the chaplain's voice; what actually moved sits under the option.
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0082.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:ec_letter_tenet_1
				scope:ec_council.var:first_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
			}
			custom_tooltip = ecumenical_council_events.0082.a.tt.tenet_1
			show_as_tooltip = {
				ecumenical_council_personal_tenet_fulfillment_effect = {
					TOPIC = scope:ec_letter_tenet_1
					PROPOSAL = scope:ec_council.var:first_debate_proposal
					SWAPPED = scope:ec_council.var:core_tenet_to_swap_1
					RITE = scope:ec_council_faith.main_rite
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:ec_letter_tenet_2
				scope:ec_council.var:second_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
			}
			custom_tooltip = ecumenical_council_events.0082.a.tt.tenet_2
			show_as_tooltip = {
				ecumenical_council_personal_tenet_fulfillment_effect = {
					TOPIC = scope:ec_letter_tenet_2
					PROPOSAL = scope:ec_council.var:second_debate_proposal
					SWAPPED = scope:ec_council.var:core_tenet_to_swap_2
					RITE = scope:ec_council_faith.main_rite
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:ec_letter_doctrine_1
				scope:ec_council.var:third_debate_resolution ?= flag:passed
			}
			custom_tooltip = ecumenical_council_events.0082.a.tt.doctrine_1
		}
	}
}

##########################################
## Third Debate per-doctrine-group stance picks.
## Each event is triggered directly from the activity's Third Debate phase on_enter (host branch) based on the
## group of scope:ec_debate_topic_3. The host picks a stance (change / keep / strengthen)
## which is stored in scope:activity.var:current_debate_proposal and read by the guest event 0031.
##########################################

# Third Debate - doctrine_marriage_type (monogamy / polygamy / concubines)
ecumenical_council_events.0050 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0050.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0050.desc.intro
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0050.desc.doctrine_marriage_type_description
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:ec_chaplain_pick
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0050.desc.chaplain_suggestion
		}
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = worry
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_portrait
		animation = preaching_no_book
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:court_chaplain
		animation = thinking
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council

	immediate = {
		# For MP
		ecumenical_council_busy_effect = yes
		save_major_scopes_effect = yes
		doctrine:doctrine_monogamy = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_monogamy
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_monogamy_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_monogamy = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_polygamy = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_polygamy
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_polygamy_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_polygamy = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_concubines = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_concubines
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_concubines_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_concubines = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}

		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monogamy }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_polygamy }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_concubines }
	}

	# Push for monogamy
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0050.doctrine_monogamy
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monogamy
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_monogamy }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monogamy
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_monogamy = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_monogamy }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_monogamy_value
		}
	}
	# Push for polygamy
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0050.doctrine_polygamy
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_polygamy
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_polygamy }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_polygamy
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_polygamy = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_polygamy }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_polygamy_value
		}
	}
	# Push for concubines
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0050.doctrine_concubines
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_concubines
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_concubines }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_concubines
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_concubines = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_concubines }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_concubines_value
		}
	}

	after = {
		# For MP
		ecumenical_council_ready_effect = yes
		# Sort into groups and trigger the notification event for other players
		pam_sort_guests_into_ecumenical_council_groups_effect = {
			NEXT_EVENT = 0031 # ecumenical_council_events.0031
		}
	}
}

# Third Debate - doctrine_divorce (disallowed / approval / allowed)
ecumenical_council_events.0051 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0051.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0051.desc.intro
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0051.desc.doctrine_divorce_description
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:ec_chaplain_pick
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0051.desc.chaplain_suggestion
		}
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = worry
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_portrait
		animation = preaching_no_book
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:court_chaplain
		animation = thinking
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council

	immediate = {
		ecumenical_council_busy_effect = yes
		save_major_scopes_effect = yes
		doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_divorce_disallowed
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_divorce_disallowed_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_divorce_approval
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_divorce_approval_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_divorce_allowed = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_divorce_allowed
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_divorce_allowed_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_divorce_allowed = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}

		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_divorce_disallowed }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_divorce_approval }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_divorce_allowed }
	}

	# Push for divorce disallowed
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0051.doctrine_divorce_disallowed
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_divorce_disallowed }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_divorce_disallowed_value
		}
	}
	# Push for divorce by approval
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0051.doctrine_divorce_approval
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_divorce_approval }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_divorce_approval_value
		}
	}
	# Push for divorce allowed
	option = {

# Third Debate - doctrine_consanguinity (dynastic / restricted / cousins / aunt_nephew_and_uncle_niece / unrestricted)

			save_scope_as = doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic

		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_restricted = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {

				doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_unrestricted = {

			}
		}

		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_consanguinity_restricted }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_consanguinity_cousins }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_consanguinity_aunt_nephew_and_uncle_niece }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_consanguinity_unrestricted }
	}

	# Push for lineal prohibition (same-dynasty and close/extended-family marriage forbidden)
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0060.doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }

		}

# Third Debate - doctrine_theocracy (temporal / lay_clergy)
ecumenical_council_events.0062 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0062.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0062.desc.intro
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0062.desc.doctrine_theocracy_description
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:ec_chaplain_pick
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0062.desc.chaplain_suggestion
		}
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = worry
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_portrait
		animation = preaching_no_book
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:court_chaplain
		animation = thinking
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council

	immediate = {
		ecumenical_council_busy_effect = yes
		save_major_scopes_effect = yes
		doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_theocracy_temporal_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value
			}
		}

		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_theocracy_temporal }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy }
	}

	# redirect to required doctrine if you have no option here.
	trigger = {
		faith.main_rite = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy }
				rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal
			}
			trigger_else = {
				rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy
			}
		}
	}
	on_trigger_fail = {
		# i.e. you have doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy + doctrine_temporal_head.
		trigger_event = ecumenical_council_events.0063 # go hof doctrine instead.
	}

	# Push for temporal theocracy
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0062.doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_temporal }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_theocracy_temporal_value
		}
	}
	# Push for lay clergy
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0062.doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy_value
		}
	}

	after = {
		ecumenical_council_ready_effect = yes
		# Sort into groups and trigger the notification event for other players
		pam_sort_guests_into_ecumenical_council_groups_effect = {
			NEXT_EVENT = 0031 # ecumenical_council_events.0031
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
	# We adopt conditional appointment so the Investiture cycle can start again
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0067.doctrine_clerical_succession_conditional
		trigger = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			scope:host.faith = {
				NOT = { has_doctrine = doctrine_clerical_succession_conditional }
				has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_regions
			}
			NOT = {
				situation:the_christian_church ?= {
					has_situation_top_phase_parameter = the_christian_church_investiture_controversy
				}
			}
		}

		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_clerical_succession_conditional }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 25 # Tunable: AI willingness to restart the Investiture cycle by adopting Conditional.
		}
	}

	after = {
		ecumenical_council_ready_effect = yes
		# Sort into groups and trigger the notification event for other players
		pam_sort_guests_into_ecumenical_council_groups_effect = {
			NEXT_EVENT = 0031 # ecumenical_council_events.0031
		}
	}
}

		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_polytheist }
	}
	
	# Push for monotheism
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0071.doctrine_monotheist
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monotheist
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_monotheist }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monotheist
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_monotheist = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_monotheist }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_monotheist_value
		}
	}
	# Push for polytheism
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0071.doctrine_polytheist
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_polytheist
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_polytheist }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_polytheist
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_polytheist = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_polytheist }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_polytheist_value
		}
	}

	after = {
		ecumenical_council_ready_effect = yes
		# Sort into groups and trigger the notification event for other players
		pam_sort_guests_into_ecumenical_council_groups_effect = {
			NEXT_EVENT = 0031 # ecumenical_council_events.0031
		}
	}
}

# Third Debate - monasticism_group (encouraged / accepted / absent / forbidden)
ecumenical_council_events.0072 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0072.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0072.desc.intro
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0072.desc.doctrine_monasticism_description
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				exists = scope:ec_chaplain_pick
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0072.desc.chaplain_suggestion
		}
	}
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = worry
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_portrait
		animation = preaching_no_book
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:court_chaplain
		animation = thinking
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council

	immediate = {
		ecumenical_council_busy_effect = yes
		save_major_scopes_effect = yes
		doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_monasticism_encouraged
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = doctrine_monasticism_encouraged_value
			value = {
				value = 0
				doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = doctrine
				}
				add = ecumenical_council_doctrine_host_pick_value

		doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_accepted = {
			save_scope_as = doctrine_monasticism_accepted
		}

		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_accepted }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_absent }
		ec_chaplain_consider_doctrine_effect = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_forbidden }
	}

	# Push for monasticism encouraged
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0072.doctrine_monasticism_encouraged
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_monasticism_encouraged_value
		}
	}
	# Push for monasticism accepted
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0072.doctrine_monasticism_accepted
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_accepted
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_accepted }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_accepted
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_accepted = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_accepted }
		ai_will_select = {
			value = 100
			add = scope:doctrine_monasticism_accepted_value
		}
	}
	# Push for monasticism absent
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0072.doctrine_monasticism_absent
		trigger = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_absent
			faith.main_rite = { rite_can_choose_doctrine = doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_absent }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			scope:ec_debate_topic_3 != doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_absent
		}
		doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_absent = { save_scope_as = ec_proposal_doctrine }
		ecumenical_council_set_proposal_effect = { PROPOSAL = doctrine:doctrine_monasticism_absent }
		ai_will_select = {

	# Push for monasticism forbidden

#################################################
## ecumenical_council_events.0101-0110 - Themed "sway the group" debate-phase pulse events
## Each event groups swayable attendees by a shared feature (the premise) and reuses
## ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect / ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect.
## As host you may win the grouped Conservatives over to the change; as a guest you may do the
## same or instead turn the grouped Innovators against it.
#################################################

# The Learned - attendees who weigh doctrine by scholarship and scripture
ecumenical_council_events.0101 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0101.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = page_flipping
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_learned_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_learned_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_learned_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_learned_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_learned_trigger }
	}

	# Win the learned over to the change (Conservatives -> Innovators)
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0101.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = shrewd
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0101.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = shrewd }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
			learning >= high_skill_rating
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = shrewd }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		add_piety = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0101.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	# Guests may turn the learned against the change (Innovators -> Conservatives)
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0101.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = shrewd
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0101.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = shrewd }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_innovators_value
			learning >= high_skill_rating
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = shrewd }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		add_piety = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0101.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0101.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0101.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# The Devout - attendees of fierce zeal or high piety
ecumenical_council_events.0102 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0102.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0102.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = preaching_no_book
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = personality_zealous
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_devout_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_devout_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_devout_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_devout_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_devout_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0102.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = zealous
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0102.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			cynical = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0102.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = zealous }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = zealous }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0102.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		add_piety = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			cynical = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0102.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0102.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0102.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0102.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0102.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0102.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0102.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = zealous
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0102.innovators_custom

			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0

	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0102.oppose_duel

				name = conservative
			}

		duel = {

				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.

					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49

			}

# The Worldly - worldly conservatives demand patronage; innovator face need not be worldly
ecumenical_council_events.0103 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0103.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0103.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = personality_greedy
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = anger
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# The desc names the lead conservative's title, so the premise needs a titled worldly conservative
			# Innovator face can be any innovator (or the host)
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_worldly_trigger = yes
				exists = primary_title
			}
			# So the player is never shown as their own lead innovator
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
				}
				scope:host != root
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_worldly_trigger }
		# Oppose flips innovators of any kind; expand past the worldly filter used for conservatives.
		scope:activity = {
			every_guest_subset = {
				name = innovator
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { is_in_list = feature_innovators }
						this != scope:host
						is_ai = yes
					}
					add_to_list = feature_innovators
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = feature_innovators_size
			value = list_size:feature_innovators
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = feature_innovators_size_halved
			value = {
				value = list_size:feature_innovators
				multiply = 0.5
				floor = yes
			}
		}
		# The desc names the lead conservative's title, so a titleless lead is recast
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					scope:lead_conservative ?= { exists = primary_title }
				}
			}
			clear_saved_scope = lead_conservative
			scope:activity = {
				ordered_attending_character = {
					order_by = highest_held_title_tier
					limit = {
						this != root
						is_ai = yes
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
						ecumenical_council_is_worldly_trigger = yes
						exists = primary_title
					}
					save_scope_as = lead_conservative
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
			}
			scope:activity = {
				ordered_attending_character = {
					order_by = highest_held_title_tier
					limit = {
						this != root
						is_ai = yes
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:lead_conservative
							}
							this != scope:lead_conservative
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = lead_innovator
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
				# Do not cast the viewer as their own lead innovator.
				scope:host != root
			}
			scope:host = {
				save_scope_as = lead_innovator
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0103.support
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_simony
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0103.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_simony }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}

		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = {
			value = medium_gold_value
			multiply = ecumenical_council_sway_change_cost_multiplier_value
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0103.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = stewardship
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0103.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0103.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0103.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0103.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_simony
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0103.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_simony }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}

		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0103.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = stewardship
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0103.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0103.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0103.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
