# Your Kin - attendees of your own dynasty
ecumenical_council_events.0104 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0104.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = admiration
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
			scope:lead_innovator != root
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = happiness
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		# A lowborn root has no dynasty, and the dynasty comparisons below would match every other lowborn
		is_lowborn = no
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				dynasty = root.dynasty
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					dynasty = root.dynasty
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { dynasty = root.dynasty }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_dynasty_member_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_dynasty_member_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0104.support
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_filial_piety
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0104.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_filial_piety }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_filial_piety }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0104.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0104.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_filial_piety
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0104.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_filial_piety }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_filial_piety }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0104.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0104.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0104.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# Your Countrymen - attendees of your own culture
ecumenical_council_events.0105 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0105.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0105.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = personality_honorable
		camera = camera_event_center_moved_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = happiness
		camera = camera_event_very_right_fov35
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				culture = root.culture
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					culture = root.culture
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { culture = root.culture }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_same_culture_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_same_culture_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0105.support
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_communal_identity
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0105.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0105.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_communal_identity }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_communal_identity }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0105.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0105.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0105.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0105.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0105.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0105.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0105.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0105.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_communal_identity
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}

			LIST = feature_innovators

			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {

# Your Vassals - attendees who owe you fealty
ecumenical_council_events.0106 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0106.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			#Innovator
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						this = scope:host
						is_in_guest_subset = {
							name = innovator
						}
					}
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0106.desc_innovator
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0106.desc
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = obsequious_bow
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = personality_honorable
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme (host cannot satisfy this filter).
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_vassal_trigger = yes
			}
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				ecumenical_council_is_vassal_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_vassal_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_vassal_trigger }
	}

		name = ecumenical_council_events.0106.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = just

				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0106.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0106.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = just }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host

					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			learning >= high_skill_rating
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = just }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0106.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0106.support_duel

				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = stewardship
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value

					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0106.support_duel.success

				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0106.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0106.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0106.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0106.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0106.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# The Rising Generation - young attendees eager to make their name
ecumenical_council_events.0107 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0107.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0107.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = disapproval
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = happiness
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_young_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_young_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_young_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_young_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_young_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0107.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = ambitious
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0107.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0107.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = ambitious }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = ambitious }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0107.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0107.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0107.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0107.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0107.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0107.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0107.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0107.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = ambitious
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0107.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0107.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = ambitious }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = ambitious }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0107.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {

				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1

				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0107.oppose_duel.success

# Friends and Confidants - a friend offers to vote with you; take the promise or release them
ecumenical_council_events.0108 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0108.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0108.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:friend
		}
		character = scope:friend
		animation = admiration
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			# Host/innovator: need friend conservatives to pull onto the change
			AND = {
				OR = {
					this = scope:host
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
				}
				scope:activity = {
					any_attending_character = {
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
						is_ai = yes
						this != root
						NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
						ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			# Conservative guest: need friend innovators to pull against the change
			AND = {
				this != scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				scope:activity = {
					any_attending_character = {
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
						is_ai = yes
						this != root
						ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger }
		# Speaking friend is the lead of the camp you can actually pull.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					this = scope:host
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:lead_conservative
				}
				scope:lead_conservative = {
					save_scope_as = friend
				}
			}
			else = {
				scope:activity = {
					ordered_attending_character = {
						order_by = highest_held_title_tier
						limit = {
							this != root
							is_ai = yes
							is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
							ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger = yes
						}
						save_scope_as = friend
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				this != scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:lead_innovator
				}
				scope:lead_innovator = {
					save_scope_as = friend
				}
			}
			else = {
				scope:activity = {
					ordered_attending_character = {
						order_by = highest_held_title_tier
						limit = {
							this != root
							is_ai = yes
							is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
							ecumenical_council_is_confidant_trigger = yes
						}
						save_scope_as = friend
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Take the promise: friends join your side of the vote
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0108.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0108.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0108.a
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0108.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	# Release them: no sway, stress relief for not leaning on the bond
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0108.b
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_trait = content
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 25
				has_trait = compassionate
			}
		}
	}
}

# The Like-Minded - attendees who already chafe at some doctrine of the faith
ecumenical_council_events.0109 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0109.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0109.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council

		character = root
		animation = interested

	}

		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = scheme
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}

	}

				ecumenical_council_has_desired_tenet_trigger = yes

		duel = {
			skill = intrigue
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0109.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0109.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0109.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0109.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0109.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# The Ambitious - attendees hungry for patronage and advancement
ecumenical_council_events.0110 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0110.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0110.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = admiration
		camera = camera_event_center_moved_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = scheme
		camera = camera_event_very_right_fov35
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				has_trait = ambitious
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					has_trait = ambitious
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { has_trait = ambitious }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_ambitious_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_ambitious_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0110.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = generous
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0110.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0110.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = generous }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = generous }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0110.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = feature_conservatives
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0110.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = intrigue
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0110.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0110.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0110.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0110.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0110.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0110.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = generous
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0110.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0110.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = generous }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = generous }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0110.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = feature_innovators
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40

				compare_modifier = {

# The Elders
ecumenical_council_events.0111 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0111.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0111.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = personality_zealous
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_elderly_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_elderly_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_elderly_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_elderly_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_elderly_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0111.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = patient

					name = innovator
				}
			}

			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0111.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0111.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = patient }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			learning >= high_skill_rating
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = patient }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0111.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0111.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1

				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives

# The Plague-Struck

	}

		trigger = {

					name = innovator

			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value

# The Besieged Faithful
ecumenical_council_events.0113 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0113.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0113.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_has_besieged_faithful_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_has_besieged_faithful_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_has_besieged_faithful_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_has_besieged_faithful_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_has_besieged_faithful_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0113.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = brave
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0113.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0113.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = brave }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = brave }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_piety = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0113.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0113.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = martial
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0113.support_duel.success

				}
			}

				}

					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0113.conservatives_custom_halved

# The Warriors
ecumenical_council_events.0114 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0114.t
	desc = {
		desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.desc.intro
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					exists = scope:ec_hostile_faith
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.desc.conservative
			}
			desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.desc.conservative_generic
		}
	}
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = personality_bold
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_warrior_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_warrior_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_warrior_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_warrior_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_warrior_trigger }
		# Prefer a neighboring realm faith hostile to ROOT; else a pagan faith; else a Muslim faith.
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_neighboring_and_across_water_top_liege_realm_owner = {
					faith = {
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_neighboring_and_across_water_top_liege_realm_owner = {
				limit = {
					faith = {
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
				}
				faith = { save_scope_as = ec_hostile_faith }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_religion_global = {
					is_in_family = rf_pagan
					any_faith = {
						this != root.faith
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_religion_global = {
				limit = {
					is_in_family = rf_pagan
					any_faith = {
						this != root.faith
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
				}
				random_faith = {
					limit = {
						this != root.faith
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = ec_hostile_faith
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				religion:islam_religion = {
					any_faith = {
						this != root.faith
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
				}
			}
			religion:islam_religion = {
				random_faith = {
					limit = {
						this != root.faith
						faith_hostility_level = {
							target = root.faith
							value >= faith_hostile_level
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = ec_hostile_faith
				}
			}
		}
		# Loc names both leads; force cast picks if the feature lists alone do not supply them.
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0114.support
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_warmonger
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0114.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_warmonger }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_warmonger }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0114.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = martial
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0114.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_warmonger
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0114.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_warmonger }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_warmonger }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0114.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = martial
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0114.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0114.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

# The Cunning
ecumenical_council_events.0115 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0115.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0115.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_high_intrigue_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_high_intrigue_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_high_intrigue_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_high_intrigue_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_high_intrigue_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0115.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = deceitful
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0115.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0115.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = deceitful }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = deceitful }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0115.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0115.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = intrigue
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0115.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0115.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0115.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0115.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0115.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

		trigger = {

				name = conservative
			}
		}

			LIST = feature_innovators

		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_high_diplomacy_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_high_diplomacy_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_high_diplomacy_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_high_diplomacy_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_high_diplomacy_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0117.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = gregarious
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0117.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0117.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = gregarious }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = gregarious }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0117.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0117.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0117.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0117.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0117.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0117.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0117.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0117.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = gregarious
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0117.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0117.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = gregarious }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = gregarious }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0

	}
	option = {

			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative

		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating

					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5

				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.

					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value

			}

				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.

# Distant Custom
ecumenical_council_events.0118 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0118.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_conservative
		}
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = personality_zealous
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Distant conservatives are the theme; innovator preacher need not be distant.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_distant_custom_trigger = yes
			}
			# A distinct innovator face must exist so the player is never shown as their own innovator
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
				}
				scope:host != root
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_distant_custom_trigger }
		# Oppose flips innovators of any kind; expand past the distant filter used for conservatives.
		scope:activity = {
			every_guest_subset = {
				name = innovator
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { is_in_list = feature_innovators }
						this != scope:host
						is_ai = yes
					}
					add_to_list = feature_innovators
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = feature_innovators_size
			value = list_size:feature_innovators
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = feature_innovators_size_halved
			value = {
				value = list_size:feature_innovators
				multiply = 0.5
				floor = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					scope:lead_conservative ?= {
						ecumenical_council_is_distant_custom_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			clear_saved_scope = lead_conservative
			scope:activity = {
				ordered_attending_character = {
					order_by = highest_held_title_tier
					limit = {
						this != root
						is_ai = yes
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
						ecumenical_council_is_distant_custom_trigger = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = lead_conservative
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
			}
			scope:activity = {
				ordered_attending_character = {
					order_by = highest_held_title_tier
					limit = {
						this != root
						is_ai = yes
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:lead_conservative
							}
							this != scope:lead_conservative
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = lead_innovator
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:lead_innovator }
				# Do not cast the viewer as their own lead innovator.
				scope:host != root
			}
			scope:host = {
				save_scope_as = lead_innovator
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0118.support
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_adaptive
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0118.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_adaptive }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			learning >= high_skill_rating
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_adaptive }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0118.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0118.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_personal_tenet = tenet_adaptive
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0118.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_adaptive }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
			learning >= high_skill_rating
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_personal_tenet = tenet_adaptive }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0118.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0118.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0118.oppose_duel.failure
