# The Glorious
ecumenical_council_events.0120 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0120.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				prestige_level >= high_prestige_level
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					prestige_level >= high_prestige_level
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { prestige_level >= high_prestige_level }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_prestigious_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_prestigious_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0120.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = arrogant
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0120.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = arrogant }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = arrogant }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0120.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0120.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = arrogant
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0120.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = arrogant }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = arrogant }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0120.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0120.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0120.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# The Ailing
ecumenical_council_events.0122 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0122.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme; host may front innovators only if themed.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				ecumenical_council_is_ailing_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				any_attending_character = {
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
					is_ai = yes
					this != root
					ecumenical_council_is_ailing_trigger = yes
				}
				AND = {
					scope:host != root
					scope:host = { ecumenical_council_is_ailing_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_ailing_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_ailing_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0122.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = compassionate
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			scope:feature_conservatives_size >= 1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0122.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			scope:feature_conservatives_size >= 1
			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			scope:feature_conservatives_size >= 1
		}
		add_piety = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0122.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			scope:feature_conservatives_size >= 1
		}
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0122.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = compassionate
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			scope:feature_innovators_size >= 1
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0122.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			scope:feature_innovators_size >= 1
			piety >= ecumenical_council_sway_piety_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			scope:feature_innovators_size >= 1
		}
		add_piety = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0122.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			scope:feature_innovators_size >= 1
		}
		duel = {
			skill = learning
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0122.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0122.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {

	option = {

# The Allied
ecumenical_council_events.0123 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0123.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0123.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			root != scope:host
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Both debate faces must match the theme (host cannot satisfy this filter).

				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }

				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				is_allied_to = root
			}
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				is_allied_to = root
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_ally_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_ally_trigger }

					name = innovator
				}

		}

				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0123.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0123.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0123.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0123.oppose_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# The Rivals - a rival threatens to vote against you; challenge the spite, refuse to rise to it, or murder them while distracted
ecumenical_council_events.0126 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0126.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0126.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:rival
		}
		character = scope:rival
		animation = disapproval
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					this = scope:host
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
				}
				scope:activity = {
					any_attending_character = {
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
						is_ai = yes
						this != root
						NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
						ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				this != scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				scope:activity = {
					any_attending_character = {
						is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
						is_ai = yes
						this != root
						ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger }
		# Speaking rival is the lead of the camp you can actually pull.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					this = scope:host
					is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:lead_conservative
				}
				scope:lead_conservative = {
					save_scope_as = rival
				}
			}
			else = {
				scope:activity = {
					ordered_attending_character = {
						order_by = highest_held_title_tier
						limit = {
							this != root
							is_ai = yes
							is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
							ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger = yes
						}
						save_scope_as = rival
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				this != scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:lead_innovator
				}
				scope:lead_innovator = {
					save_scope_as = rival
				}
			}
			else = {
				scope:activity = {
					ordered_attending_character = {
						order_by = highest_held_title_tier
						limit = {
							this != root
							is_ai = yes
							is_in_guest_subset = { name = innovator }
							ecumenical_council_is_rival_trigger = yes
						}
						save_scope_as = rival
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Challenge the spite: pull their camp onto your side of the vote
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0126.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0126.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0126.a
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0126.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	# Refuse to rise to it
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0126.b
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_trait = content
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 25
				has_trait = compassionate
			}
		}
	}
	# Murder them while distracted
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0126.c
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:rival
			can_start_scheme = {
				type = murder
				target_character = scope:rival
			}
		}
		add_internal_flag = dangerous
		start_scheme = {
			type = murder
			target_character = scope:rival
		}
		custom_tooltip = ecumenical_council_events.0126.c.tt
		hidden_effect = {
			random_scheme = {
				limit = {
					scheme_type = murder
					scheme_target_character = scope:rival
				}
				add_scheme_modifier = {
					type = extra_success_chance_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			callous = minor_stress_impact_loss
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_trait = callous
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_trait = sadistic
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 25
				has_trait = vengeful
			}
		}
	}
}

# The Cowed
ecumenical_council_events.0129 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = ecumenical_council_events.0129.t
	desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.desc
	theme = ecumenical_council
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_crowd
	}
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:lead_conservative
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_center_pointing_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:lead_innovator
		}
		character = scope:lead_innovator
		animation = interested
	}
	cooldown = { days = 60 }

	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			# Loc fronts an intimidated conservative; require that face.
			any_attending_character = {
				is_in_guest_subset = { name = conservative }
				is_ai = yes
				this != root
				NOT = { pam_ec_is_active_counterplayer_trigger = yes }
				has_dread_level_towards = { target = root level >= 1 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_sway_guests_scopes_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_dreads_root_trigger }
		ecumenical_council_ensure_themed_leads_effect = { TRIGGER = ecumenical_council_dreads_root_trigger }
	}

	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support
		trigger = {
			has_trait = wrathful
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.conservatives_custom_capped
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_free_max
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = wrathful }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
			dread >= ecumenical_council_sway_dread_cost_conservatives_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = wrathful }
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		add_dread = {
			value = ecumenical_council_sway_dread_cost_conservatives_value
			multiply = -1
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
			LIST = feature_conservatives
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.conservatives_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				is_in_guest_subset = {
					name = innovator
				}
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = intrigue
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = -1
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.conservatives_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_innovators_effect = {
					LIST = feature_conservatives
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.conservatives_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_conservatives_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:ec_sway_duel_penalty
					multiplier = 1
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0129.oppose
		trigger = {
			has_trait = wrathful
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0129.oppose
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = wrathful }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
			prestige >= ecumenical_council_sway_prestige_cost_innovators_value
		}
		show_as_unavailable = {
			NOT = { has_trait = wrathful }
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		add_prestige = {
			value = -40
			multiply = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
			LIST = feature_innovators
			CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.innovators_custom
			MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ecumenical_council_events.0129.oppose_duel
		trigger = {
			this != scope:host
			is_in_guest_subset = {
				name = conservative
			}
		}
		duel = {
			skill = intrigue
			value = high_skill_rating
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# The debated tenet's faith (un)popularity nudges the sway in the same direction.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = -0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.oppose_duel.success
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.innovators_custom
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					scope:feature_innovators_size_halved >= 1
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.oppose_duel.success_halved
				ecumenical_council_sway_to_conservatives_effect = {
					LIST = feature_innovators
					CUSTOM = ecumenical_council_events.0129.innovators_custom_halved
					MAX = scope:feature_innovators_size_halved
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				# ...and correspondingly makes the sway less likely to fail.
				compare_modifier = {
					value = pam_ec_sway_tenet_impact_value
					multiplier = 0.1
					min = -49
				}
				desc = ecumenical_council_events.0129.support_duel.failure
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ec_sway_leave
		add_piety = 10
		stress_and_fulfillment_impact = {
			content = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}
